[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");

    end
    $fclose(fd);

    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;

[Step 3/60]
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

... (showing lines 118-282 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU), cycle-accurate\n// re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Timing scheme (all timing counted in *compute* cycles, i.e. cycles where\n// w_valid == 0; w_valid freezes the whole datapath and reloads weights):\n//\n//   * Row i of A is delayed SBASE+i = N-1+i compute cycles in an input skew\n//     shift register (zeros injected while a_valid is low).\n//   * The skewed row is BROADCAST to all N columns (no horizontal hop\n//     registers): PE(i,j) forms prod = A_row[i] * B[i][j] on the same cycle\n//     for every column j.\n//   * The partial sum flows north->south, one register hop per row.  Row i's\n//     product enters the column chain SBASE+i cycles after injection, so the\n//     8 accumulated products of result row m line up and leave the bottom of\n//     the array exactly SBASE+N = 2*N-1 = 15 compute cycles after injection --\n//     which is also when the valid pipeline (a_valid delayed 2*N-1) asserts\n//     c_valid.  Because all columns finish on the same cycle, no output\n//     de-skew registers are needed at all: c_data is the sign-extended bottom\n//     row of the array.\n//\n// Widths: a partial sum of k 8x8 signed products lies in\n// [-16256*k, 16384*k], so 19 bits signed covers the full 8-product sum\n// ([-130048, +131072]) exactly -- accumulators are 19 bits instead of 32.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW   = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32)  // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,  // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,   // weight row, {col N-1 ... col 0}\n    input                   a_valid,  // activation-row valid\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,   // activation row, {col N-1 ... col 0}\n    output                  c_valid,  // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data    // result row, {col N-1 ... col 0}\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT   = 2*N-1; // injection -> result latency (compute cycles)\n   localparam SBASE = N-1;   // base input skew\n   localparam PW    = 19;    // partial-sum width (fits +/- 8 products)\n\n   // weight-load enable / compute enable (array stalls while loading)\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed SBASE+i compute cycles, zero-padded when\n   // a_valid is low (idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_row;\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         localparam D = SBASE + i;\n         wire [DW-1:0]  din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         reg  [D*DW-1:0] sr;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     sr <= {(D*DW){1'b0}};\n           else if (en) sr <= {sr[(D-1)*DW-1:0], din};\n         assign a_row[i*DW +: DW] = sr[D*DW-1 -: DW];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Array state: stationary weights and per-PE partial sums.\n   //   w_mem[i*N+j] = weight of PE(i,j) = B[i][j]\n   //   p_mem[i*N+j] = partial sum output of PE(i,j)\n   // All held in flat vectors so the whole mesh updates in ONE process\n   // (nonblocking assignments read the pre-edge values -> correct systolic\n   // semantics for the vertical shift chains).\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [N*N*DW-1:0] w_mem;\n   reg [N*N*PW-1:0] p_mem;\n\n   // combinational per-PE products (broadcast activation, stationary weight)\n   wire [N*N*2*DW-1:0] prod_bus;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_prod_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_prod_col\n            assign prod_bus[(i*N+j)*2*DW +: 2*DW] =\n                   $signed(a_row[i*DW +: DW]) *\n                   $signed(w_mem[(i*N+j)*DW +: DW]);\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   integer ri, rj, ridx;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (ridx = 0; ridx < N*N; ridx = ridx + 1) begin\n            w_mem[ridx*DW +: DW] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            p_mem[ridx*PW +: PW] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n         end\n      end\n      else begin\n         // weight load: shift each column one row south (bottom row first\n         // arrives from the host), top row takes w_data.\n         if (ld) begin\n            for (ri = 0; ri < N; ri = ri + 1)\n              for (rj = 0; rj < N; rj = rj + 1)\n                w_mem[(ri*N+rj)*DW +: DW] <=\n                  (ri == 0) ? w_data[rj*DW +: DW]\n                            : w_mem[(ri*N+rj-N)*DW +: DW];\n         end\n         // compute: psum_south = psum_north + sign_extend(a * w)\n         if (en) begin\n            for (ri = 0; ri < N; ri = ri + 1)\n              for (rj = 0; rj < N; rj = rj + 1)\n                p_mem[(ri*N+rj)*PW +: PW] <=\n                  ((ri == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : p_mem[(ri*N+rj-N)*PW +: PW])\n                  + {{(PW-2*DW){prod_bus[(ri*N+rj)*2*DW+2*DW-1]}},\n                     prod_bus[(ri*N+rj)*2*DW +: 2*DW]};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Result: bottom row of the array, sign-extended to ACCW.  All columns are\n   // valid on the same cycle (2*N-1 after injection), so no de-skew needed.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_out\n         wire [PW-1:0] col = p_mem[((N-1)*N+j)*PW +: PW];\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){col[PW-1]}}, col};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n     if (rst)     vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n     else if (en) vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
I've found the root cause: the golden behavior depends on the **anti-diagonal stall boundary** — in the original, PE(i,j)'s product is captured at line `T+i+j`, so a mid-flight weight reload splits old/new weights along `i+j = const`. My broadcast scheme splits by row instead. The horizontal hop structure is therefore mandatory. Let me rewrite keeping the original topology but with narrowed 19-bit accumulators/deskew (values provably fit) and async reset.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimization vs the original: the accumulators only need to hold sums of\n// k <= N signed 8x8 products, which lie in [-16256k, 16384k]; for k = 8 that\n// is [-130048, +131072], exactly fitting 19 signed bits.  Partial sums and\n// the output de-skew chain therefore use PW = 19 bits instead of 32, and\n// c_data is formed by sign extension (checked only while c_valid, where the\n// value is a true result row).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,    // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)   // partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,       // async, active high\n    input                  ld,        // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,        // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,   // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,    // activation in from west\n    input  [PW-1:0]        psum_north,// partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,   // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]    a_east,    // activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]    psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north + {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   // partial-sum width: sum of N signed (DW x DW) products\n   localparam PW   = $clog2((N << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1; // = 19 for N=8, DW=8\n   localparam LAT  = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (PW-wide accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
Target ≤500 saturates (identical results); 900 is worse. The structure is the lever now. Let me implement per-row accumulator widths (rows 0..6 can be narrower than 19):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimization vs the original: a partial sum of k <= N signed 8x8 products\n// lies in [-16256k, +16384k], so row i (which holds i+1 products) needs only\n// pw(i+1) = clog2(16384*(i+1)+1)+1 bits: 16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19 instead of\n// 32.  The de-skew chain holds full sums (19 bits).  c_data is the sign\n// extension of the 19-bit result, checked only while c_valid.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW  = 19,  // mesh net width (sign-extended storage)\n    parameter PNW = 1,   // meaningful width of psum_north\n    parameter POW = 16)  // meaningful width of psum_south\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,       // async, active high\n    input                  ld,        // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,        // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,   // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,    // activation in from west\n    input  [PW-1:0]        psum_north,// partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,   // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]    a_east,    // activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]    psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   // exact-width MAC: PNW-bit incoming partial (sign-extended to POW) plus\n   // the sign-extended product; result sign-extended to PW for the mesh net\n   // (upper bits are dropped by synthesis, they have no readers).\n   wire [PNW-1:0] pn   = psum_north[PNW-1:0];\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum  = $signed(pn) + $signed(prod);\n   wire [PW-1:0]  sums = $signed(sum);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sums;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   // accumulator width for a partial sum of k signed (DW x DW) products\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;    // full-sum width for N=8, DW=8\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (per-row-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_prw.sv')

Per-row widths alone: delay dropped 7% but area *rose* (likely port-boundary bits kept + abc re-balancing). Let me build the principled version: an exact-width flat mesh so every port and flop is minimal, with no redundant sign-fill bits:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations vs the original:\n//   * A partial sum of k <= N signed 8x8 products lies in\n//     [-16256k, +16384k], so row i (holding i+1 products) needs only\n//     pw(i+1) = clog2(16384*(i+1)+1)+1 bits: 16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19\n//     instead of 32 everywhere.  The mesh is a flat exact-width vector so\n//     every flop/port carries only the bits it needs.\n//   * The de-skew chain holds full sums (19 bits), sign-extended to ACCW.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PNW = 1,   // width of incoming partial sum\n    parameter POW = 16)  // width of outgoing partial sum\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]    a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <=                rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   // accumulator width for a partial sum of k signed (DW x DW) products\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // mesh layout: column j occupies [j*COLW +: COLW]; within a column the\n   // field entering row i (width pw(i)) starts at rowoff(i).\n   localparam COLW = (N == 0) ? 1 : pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                              + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff; // byte offset of the field entering row i\n      input integer i;\n      integer wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1))) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;    // full-sum width for N=8, DW=8\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j [ASSISTANT]
prw@650 remains best (3.105M). Now a structural experiment: replace `$signed*$signed` with an explicit gated-shift partial-product tree, in case Yosys's default `$mul` techmap bloats the cone with sign-extended operands:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n// (prw = per-row accumulator widths, mul = explicit partial-product product)\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// A partial sum of k <= N signed 8x8 products lies in [-16256k, +16384k],\n// so row i (holding i+1 products) needs pw(i+1) bits: 16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19.\n//\n// The signed product is formed explicitly as gated shifted partial products\n// of the sign-extended activation:\n//   a*w = sum_{k=0..6} w[k]*a*2^k - w[7]*a*2^7   (exact, mod 2^16)\n// which keeps the multiplier cone at 8 one-term partials instead of\n// whatever operand widening the generic $mul techmap performs.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW  = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW  = 19,  // mesh net width (sign-extended storage)\n    parameter PNW = 1,   // meaningful width of psum_north\n    parameter POW = 16)  // meaningful width of psum_south\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,       // async, active high\n    input                  ld,        // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,        // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,   // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,    // activation in from west\n    input  [PW-1:0]        psum_north,// partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,   // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]    a_east,    // activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]    psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- explicit signed product -------------------------------------------\n   wire [W2-1:0] ap = {{DW{a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};   // a, sign-extended\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh0 = w[0] ? ap                       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh1 = w[1] ? {ap[W2-2:0], 1'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh2 = w[2] ? {ap[W2-3:0], 2'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh3 = w[3] ? {ap[W2-4:0], 3'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh4 = w[4] ? {ap[W2-5:0], 4'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh5 = w[5] ? {ap[W2-6:0], 5'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh6 = w[6] ? {ap[W2-7:0], 6'b0}       : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7p = {ap[W2-8:0], 7'b0};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7 = w[7] ? (~sh7p + {{(W2-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] prod = ((sh0 + sh1) + (sh2 + sh3))\n                      + ((sh4 + sh5) + (sh6 + sh7));\n\n   // ---- MAC into the (narrow) accumulator ---------------------------------\n   wire [PNW-1:0] pn   = psum_north[PNW-1:0];\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum  = $signed(pn) + $signed(prod);\n   wire [PW-1:0]  sums = $signed(sum);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sums;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   // accumulator width for a partial sum of k signed (DW x DW) products\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;    // full-sum width for N=8, DW=8\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (per-row-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_prwm.sv')

prwm@700 → **2.996M** (new best). Now the ultimate structural variant: exact-width mesh (pw2) + explicit partial-products + row-0 adder specialization + no dead column-7 hop register:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations:\n//   * Exact-width accumulators: row i holds i+1 signed 8x8 products, which\n//     lie in [-16256(i+1), +16384(i+1)], so it needs pw(i+1) bits\n//     (16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19).  The mesh is a flat exact-width vector:\n//     every flop and port carries only the bits it needs.\n//   * Row 0 has no incoming partial sum (constant 0): its PE skips the add.\n//   * The last column never forwards its activation east: no dead hop reg.\n//   * The signed product is an explicit gated-shift partial-product tree:\n//       a*w = sum_{k=0..6} w[k]*a*2^k - w[7]*a*2^7   (exact, mod 2^16)\n//   * The de-skew chain holds full sums (19 bits), sign-extended to ACCW.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,  // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PNW  = 1,  // width of incoming partial sum\n    parameter POW  = 16, // width of outgoing partial sum\n    parameter TOP  = 1,  // 1 = no incoming partial sum (row 0)\n    parameter EAST = 1)  // 1 = activation hop register needed\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output [DW-1:0]        a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- explicit signed product: gated shifted partial products ----------\n   wire [W2-1:0] ap = {{DW{a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};   // a, sign-extended\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh0 = w[0] ? ap                 : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh1 = w[1] ? {ap[W2-2:0], 1'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh2 = w[2] ? {ap[W2-3:0], 2'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh3 = w[3] ? {ap[W2-4:0], 3'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh4 = w[4] ? {ap[W2-5:0], 4'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh5 = w[5] ? {ap[W2-6:0], 5'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh6 = w[6] ? {ap[W2-7:0], 6'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7p = {ap[W2-8:0], 7'b0};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7 = w[7] ? (~sh7p + {{(W2-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] prod = ((sh0 + sh1) + (sh2 + sh3))\n                      + ((sh4 + sh5) + (sh6 + sh7));\n\n   // ---- MAC (skipped for row 0, whose incoming sum is constant 0) --------\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum  = TOP ? $signed(prod)\n                             : $signed(psum_north) + $signed(prod);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {POW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en)\n           psum_south <= sum;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- activation hop register (absent in the last column) --------------\n   generate\n      if (EAST) begin : g_east\n         reg [DW-1:0] a_east_r;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     a_east_r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n           else if (en) a_east_r <= a_west;\n         assign a_east = a_east_r;\n      end\n      else begin : g_noeast\n         assign a_east = {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   // accumulator width for a partial sum of k signed (DW x DW) products\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   // mesh layout: column j occupies [j*COLW +: COLW]; within a column the\n   // field entering row i (width pw(i)) starts at rowoff(i).\n   localparam COLW = pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                   + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff;\n      input integer i;\n      integer r, s;\n      begin\n         s = 0;\n         for (r = 1; r < i; r = r + 1)\n           s = s + pw(r);\n         rowoff = s;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(1), .POW(pw(1)),\n                        .TOP(1), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (1'b0),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1)),\n                        .TOP(0), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;    // full-sum width for N=8, DW=8\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (exact-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_best.sv')
