Targets below 650 are clamped (identical mapping). Now the surgical prefix-adder experiment: replace only the final MAC adder with an explicit Sklansky prefix structure (keeping the mul tree as-is):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations vs the original:\n//   * Exact-width accumulators (row i holds i+1 products -> pw(i+1) bits:\n//     16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19); flat exact-width mesh.\n//   * Row 0 skips the add (incoming sum is constant 0); last column has no\n//     activation hop register.\n//   * Explicit gated-shift partial-product tree for the signed product.\n//   * Sklansky parallel-prefix (padd) for the final MAC addition, which is\n//     the critical carry-propagate path.\n//#############################################################################\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Sklansky parallel-prefix adder:  S = A + B.\n// GI/PI in, GO/PO out, sum = (A^B) ^ {carry, 0}.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule padd\n  #(parameter W = 19)\n   (\n    input  [W-1:0] A,\n    input  [W-1:0] B,\n    output [W-1:0] S\n    );\n\n   localparam M = $clog2(W);   // prefix levels (2^M >= W)\n\n   wire [W-1:0] GI = A & B;\n   wire [W-1:0] PI = A | B;\n   wire [W-1:0] PT = A ^ B;\n\n   wire [W-1:0] GX [0:M];\n   wire [W-1:0] PX [0:M];\n\n   assign GX[0] = GI;\n   assign PX[0] = PI;\n\n   genvar l, k, i;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= M; l = l + 1) begin : g_lvl\n         for (k = 0; k < (1 << (M - l)); k = k + 1) begin : g_grp\n            for (i = 0; i < (1 << (l - 1)); i = i + 1) begin : g_bit\n               if ((k << l) + i < W) begin : g_white\n                  assign GX[l][(k<<l)+i] = GX[l-1][(k<<l)+i];\n                  assign PX[l][(k<<l)+i] = PX[l-1][(k<<l)+i];\n               end\n               if ((k << l) + (1 << (l-1)) + i < W) begin : g_black\n                  localparam T = (k << l) + (1 << (l-1)) + i;\n                  localparam R = (k << l) + (1 << (l-1)) - 1;\n                  assign GX[l][T] = GX[l-1][T] | (PX[l-1][T] & GX[l-1][R]);\n                  assign PX[l][T] = PX[l-1][T] & PX[l-1][R];\n               end\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   assign S = PT ^ {GX[M][W-2:0], 1'b0};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter PNW  = 1,\n    parameter POW  = 16,\n    parameter TOP  = 1,\n    parameter EAST = 1)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output [DW-1:0]        a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- explicit signed product: gated shifted partial products ----------\n   wire [W2-1:0] ap = {{DW{a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};   // a, sign-extended\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh0 = w[0] ? ap                 : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh1 = w[1] ? {ap[W2-2:0], 1'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh2 = w[2] ? {ap[W2-3:0], 2'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh3 = w[3] ? {ap[W2-4:0], 3'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh4 = w[4] ? {ap[W2-5:0], 4'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh5 = w[5] ? {ap[W2-6:0], 5'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh6 = w[6] ? {ap[W2-7:0], 6'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7p = {ap[W2-8:0], 7'b0};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7 = w[7] ? (~sh7p + {{(W2-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] prod = ((sh0 + sh1) + (sh2 + sh3))\n                      + ((sh4 + sh5) + (sh6 + sh7));\n\n   // ---- MAC: parallel-prefix addition (skipped for row 0) ----------------\n   wire [POW-1:0] ext_p    = $signed(psum_north);\n   wire [POW-1:0] ext_prod = $signed(prod);\n   wire [POW-1:0] mac;\n   padd #(.W(POW)) u_mac (.A(ext_p), .B(ext_prod), .S(mac));\n\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum = TOP ? $signed(prod) : mac;\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {POW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en)\n           psum_south <= sum;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- activation hop register (absent in the last column) --------------\n   generate\n      if (EAST) begin : g_east\n         reg [DW-1:0] a_east_r;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     a_east_r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n           else if (en) a_east_r <= a_west;\n         assign a_east = a_east_r;\n      end\n      else begin : g_noeast\n         assign a_east = {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam COLW = pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                   + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff;\n      input integer i;\n      integer r, s;\n      begin\n         s = 0;\n         for (r = 1; r < i; r = r + 1)\n           s = s + pw(r);\n         rowoff = s;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(1), .POW(pw(1)),\n                        .TOP(1), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (1'b0),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1)),\n                        .TOP(0), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (exact-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_pre.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
Now the CSA (carry-save) variant: replace the tree-of-ripples + MAC with an explicit 9-term carry-save reduction into a single final add — minimal gates, shallow front-end:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations vs the original:\n//   * Exact-width accumulators (row i holds i+1 products -> pw(i+1) bits:\n//     16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19); flat exact-width mesh.\n//   * Row 0 skips the add (incoming sum is constant 0); last column has no\n//     activation hop register.\n//   * The MAC is one explicit 9-term carry-save reduction (8 gated-shift\n//     partial products of the signed product + the incoming partial sum)\n//     followed by a single final carry-propagate add.  All terms are exact\n//     signed values (|a*2^k| <= 16384 fits in 16 bits), the network works\n//     mod 2^Q with Q = POW+4, and the true sum fits in Q bits, so the low\n//     POW bits are exact.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter PNW  = 1,\n    parameter POW  = 16,\n    parameter TOP  = 1,\n    parameter EAST = 1)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output [DW-1:0]        a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n   localparam Q  = POW + 4;   // carry-save network width (exact mod 2^Q)\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- exact signed partial products: gated shifted activations ---------\n   wire [W2-1:0] ap = {{DW{a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};   // a, sign-extended\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh0 = w[0] ? ap                 : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh1 = w[1] ? {ap[W2-2:0], 1'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh2 = w[2] ? {ap[W2-3:0], 2'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh3 = w[3] ? {ap[W2-4:0], 3'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh4 = w[4] ? {ap[W2-5:0], 4'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh5 = w[5] ? {ap[W2-6:0], 5'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh6 = w[6] ? {ap[W2-7:0], 6'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7p = {ap[W2-8:0], 7'b0};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7 = w[7] ? (~sh7p + {{(W2-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : {W2{1'b0}};\n\n   // terms, sign-extended to the network width\n   wire [Q-1:0] t0 = $signed(sh0);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t1 = $signed(sh1);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t2 = $signed(sh2);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t3 = $signed(sh3);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t4 = $signed(sh4);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t5 = $signed(sh5);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t6 = $signed(sh6);\n   wire [Q-1:0] t7 = $signed(sh7);\n   wire [Q-1:0] tp = TOP ? {Q{1'b0}} : $signed(psum_north);\n\n   // ---- level 1: three (3:2) reductions ----------------------------------\n   wire [Q-1:0] u0s = t0 ^ t1 ^ t2;\n   wire [Q-1:0] u0g = (t0 & t1) | (t1 & t2) | (t0 & t2);\n   wire [Q-1:0] u0c = {u0g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [Q-1:0] u1s = t3 ^ t4 ^ t5;\n   wire [Q-1:0] u1g = (t3 & t4) | (t4 & t5) | (t3 & t5);\n   wire [Q-1:0] u1c = {u1g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [Q-1:0] u2s = t6 ^ t7 ^ tp;\n   wire [Q-1:0] u2g = (t6 & t7) | (t7 & tp) | (t6 & tp);\n   wire [Q-1:0] u2c = {u2g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 2 ----------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [Q-1:0] v0s = u0s ^ u1s ^ u2s;\n   wire [Q-1:0] v0g = (u0s & u1s) | (u1s & u2s) | (u0s & u2s);\n   wire [Q-1:0] v0c = {v0g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n   wire [Q-1:0] v1s = u0c ^ u1c ^ u2c;\n   wire [Q-1:0] v1g = (u0c & u1c) | (u1c & u2c) | (u0c & u2c);\n   wire [Q-1:0] v1c = {v1g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 3 ----------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [Q-1:0] w0s = v0s ^ v1s ^ v0c;\n   wire [Q-1:0] w0g = (v0s & v1s) | (v1s & v0c) | (v0s & v0c);\n   wire [Q-1:0] w0c = {w0g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- level 4: last three terms ----------------------------------------\n   wire [Q-1:0] x0s = w0s ^ w0c ^ v1c;\n   wire [Q-1:0] x0g = (w0s & w0c) | (w0c & v1c) | (w0s & v1c);\n   wire [Q-1:0] x0c = {x0g[Q-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- single final carry-propagate add ---------------------------------\n   wire [Q-1:0] sumq = x0s + x0c;\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum = sumq[POW-1:0];\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {POW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en)\n           psum_south <= sum;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- activation hop register (absent in the last column) --------------\n   generate\n      if (EAST) begin : g_east\n         reg [DW-1:0] a_east_r;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     a_east_r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n           else if (en) a_east_r <= a_west;\n         assign a_east = a_east_r;\n      end\n      else begin : g_noeast\n         assign a_east = {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam COLW = pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                   + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff;\n      input integer i;\n      integer r, s;\n      begin\n         s = 0;\n         for (r = 1; r < i; r = r + 1)\n           s = s + pw(r);\n         rowoff = s;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(1), .POW(pw(1)),\n                        .TOP(1), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (1'b0),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1)),\n                        .TOP(0), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (exact-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if [ASSISTANT]
The CSA failed — but the error pattern reveals a classic Verilog gotcha: `TOP ? {Q{1'b0}} : $signed(psum_north)` mixes an unsigned concatenation with a signed branch, so the signed branch gets **zero**-extended (error = +2^PNW per negative partial sum). Fix: sign-extend manually, no ternary:
  -> edit_file(filename='design_csa.sv', old_string="   wire [Q-1:0] tp = TOP ? {Q{1'b0}} : $signed(psum_north);", new_string='   // explicit sign extension (a ternary with an unsigned branch would\n   // zero-extend the signed operand instead)\n   wire [Q-1:0] tp = {{(Q-PNW){psum_north[PNW-1]}}, psum_north};')

[ASSISTANT]
design_best still leads. One last structural gamble: replace the explicit shift-tree with an **unsigned 8×8 multiply + sign corrections** — `$mul` with unsigned operands may map cleaner/faster, and corrections are cheap:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations vs the original:\n//   * Exact-width accumulators (row i holds i+1 products -> pw(i+1) bits:\n//     16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19); flat exact-width mesh.\n//   * Row 0 skips the add (incoming sum is constant 0); last column has no\n//     activation hop register.\n//   * Signed product formed as an UNSIGNED 8x8 multiply plus sign\n//     corrections (mod 2^16, exact):\n//       a_s*w_s = a_u*w_u - (a[7]? w_u<<8 : 0) - (w[7]? a_u<<8 : 0)\n//     so the generic multiplier sees only unsigned operands.\n//   * The de-skew chain holds full sums (19 bits), sign-extended to ACCW.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter PNW  = 1,\n    parameter POW  = 16,\n    parameter TOP  = 1,\n    parameter EAST = 1)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output [DW-1:0]        a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- unsigned 8x8 product + two sign corrections (exact mod 2^16) -----\n   wire [W2-1:0] uum  = a_west * w;\n   wire [W2-1:0] corr = (a_west[DW-1] ? {w, {DW{1'b0}}}     : {W2{1'b0}})\n                      + (w[DW-1]      ? {a_west, {DW{1'b0}}} : {W2{1'b0}});\n   wire [W2-1:0] prod = uum - corr;\n\n   // ---- MAC into the narrow accumulator (row 0: constant incoming sum) ---\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum = TOP ? $signed(prod)\n                            : $signed(psum_north) + $signed(prod);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {POW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en)\n           psum_south <= sum;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- activation hop register (absent in the last column) --------------\n   generate\n      if (EAST) begin : g_east\n         reg [DW-1:0] a_east_r;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     a_east_r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n           else if (en) a_east_r <= a_west;\n         assign a_east = a_east_r;\n      end\n      else begin : g_noeast\n         assign a_east = {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam COLW = pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                   + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff;\n      input integer i;\n      integer r, s;\n      begin\n         s = 0;\n         for (r = 1; r < i; r = r + 1)\n           s = s + pw(r);\n         rowoff = s;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(1), .POW(pw(1)),\n                        .TOP(1), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (1'b0),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1)),\n                        .TOP(0), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (exact-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_umul.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
design_best is robust at default target too (2.9709M at 500). One last structural lottery — re-pair the partial-product tree (interleaved instead of adjacent pairs):
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU MXU)\n// Cycle-accurate re-architecture of the logikbench tpu benchmark.\n// (design_best + interleaved partial-product tree pairing)\n//\n// Topology is the canonical one (input skew i, horizontal hop j, output\n// de-skew 7-j): PE(i,j) captures its product for result-row m on compute\n// edge T+i+j.  This per-(i,j) capture schedule is REQUIRED for exact\n// equivalence -- a mid-flight weight reload must split old/new weights along\n// the anti-diagonal i+j = const, which only the hop-based dataflow produces.\n//\n// Optimizations:\n//   * Exact-width accumulators (row i holds i+1 products -> pw(i+1) bits:\n//     16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19); flat exact-width mesh.\n//   * Row 0 skips the add (incoming sum is constant 0); last column has no\n//     activation hop register.\n//   * Explicit gated-shift partial-product tree for the signed product,\n//     paired as (0,1)+(4,5) vs (2,3)+(6,7).\n//   * The de-skew chain holds full sums (19 bits), sign-extended to ACCW.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter PNW  = 1,\n    parameter POW  = 16,\n    parameter TOP  = 1,\n    parameter EAST = 1)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,        // async, active high\n    input                  ld,         // weight-load shift enable\n    input                  en,         // compute enable\n    input  [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input  [DW-1:0]        a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input  [PNW-1:0]       psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]        w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output [DW-1:0]        a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [POW-1:0]   psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam W2 = 2*DW;\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- explicit signed product: gated shifted partial products ----------\n   wire [W2-1:0] ap = {{DW{a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};   // a, sign-extended\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh0 = w[0] ? ap                 : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh1 = w[1] ? {ap[W2-2:0], 1'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh2 = w[2] ? {ap[W2-3:0], 2'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh3 = w[3] ? {ap[W2-4:0], 3'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh4 = w[4] ? {ap[W2-5:0], 4'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh5 = w[5] ? {ap[W2-6:0], 5'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh6 = w[6] ? {ap[W2-7:0], 6'b0} : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7p = {ap[W2-8:0], 7'b0};\n   wire [W2-1:0] sh7 = w[7] ? (~sh7p + {{(W2-1){1'b0}}, 1'b1}) : {W2{1'b0}};\n   wire [W2-1:0] prod = ((sh0 + sh1) + (sh4 + sh5))\n                      + ((sh2 + sh3) + (sh6 + sh7));\n\n   // ---- MAC into the narrow accumulator (row 0: constant incoming sum) ---\n   wire [POW-1:0] sum = TOP ? $signed(prod)\n                            : $signed(psum_north) + $signed(prod);\n\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {POW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en)\n           psum_south <= sum;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- activation hop register (absent in the last column) --------------\n   generate\n      if (EAST) begin : g_east\n         reg [DW-1:0] a_east_r;\n         always @(posedge clk or posedge rst)\n           if (rst)     a_east_r <= {DW{1'b0}};\n           else if (en) a_east_r <= a_west;\n         assign a_east = a_east_r;\n      end\n      else begin : g_noeast\n         assign a_east = {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N  = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter PW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     w_top,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]     a_left,\n    output [N*PW-1:0]     c_bot\n    );\n\n   function integer pw;\n      input integer k;\n      begin\n         pw = $clog2((k << (2*DW-2)) + 1) + 1;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   localparam COLW = pw(1) + pw(2) + pw(3) + pw(4)\n                   + pw(5) + pw(6) + pw(7) + pw(8);\n\n   function integer rowoff;\n      input integer i;\n      integer r, s;\n      begin\n         s = 0;\n         for (r = 1; r < i; r = r + 1)\n           s = s + pw(r);\n         rowoff = s;\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [N*COLW-1:0] psum_mesh;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*PW +: PW] =\n                psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(N) +: pw(N)];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            if (i == 0) begin : g_top\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(1), .POW(pw(1)),\n                        .TOP(1), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (1'b0),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n            else begin : g_mid\n               tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PNW(pw(i)), .POW(pw(i+1)),\n                        .TOP(0), .EAST(j != N-1)) u_pe\n                 (.clk        (clk),\n                  .rst        (rst),\n                  .ld         (ld),\n                  .en         (en),\n                  .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n                  .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n                  .psum_north (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i) +: pw(i)]),\n                  .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n                  .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n                  .psum_south (psum_mesh[j*COLW + rowoff(i+1) +: pw(i+1)]));\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N    = 8,\n    parameter DW   = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // async, active high\n    input                   w_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                   a_valid,\n    input  [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                  c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 19;\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected on gaps.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array (exact-width accumulators).\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed N-1-j cycles, PW bits, sign-extended\n   // to ACCW on the way out.\n   //##########################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed 2*N-1 compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk or posedge rst) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_alt.sv')

Final confirmation: **PASS with 0/6782 errors**. The champion is locked in.

## Summary

**Final design: `design_best.sv`** — 100% cycle-accurate on all 6,782 checks.

| | Area | Delay (ps) | **Area×Delay** |
|---|---|---|---|
| Baseline (starting_point.v) | 4604 | 1004 | 4,624,409 |
| **Final design** | **3805** | **771.7** | **2,936,200 (−36.5%)** |

**How it got there:**

1. **The key insight — the anti-diagonal capture schedule is mandatory.** My first attempt (broadcast activations, skew 7…14, zero de-skew flops) failed on mid-drain weight reloads: the golden design captures PE(i,j)'s product at compute edge `T+i+j`, so a reload splits old/new weights along `i+j = const`. Only the hop-based systolic dataflow (input skew i → horizontal hop j → output de-skew 7−j) reproduces that boundary — column 7 matched (split at row 3, same as broadcast) while lower columns diverged. I reverted to the canonical topology, which is also provably flop-minimal.

2. **Exact-width accumulators.** A partial sum of k signed 8×8 products lies in [−16256k, +16384k], so rows need only 16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19 bits — not 32. The mesh is a flat exact-width vector (every flop/port carries only needed bits), with sign-extension on `c_data`.

3. **Multiplier formulation.** The single biggest lever: replacing Yosys's default signed `$mul` (whose techmap bloats the cone) with an explicit gated-shift partial-product tree (`a*w = Σ_{k<7} w[k]·a·2^k − w[7]·a·2^7`, exact mod 2¹⁶) cut ~400 area units.

4. **Dead-logic removal:** row-0 PE skips its adder (incoming sum is constant 0); the last column's activation hop register is eliminated.

5. **Explored and rejected:** prefix/Sklansky adders (abc re-canonicalizes equivalent functions), unsigned-mul+corrections (worse both metrics), CSA front-end (faster but +336 area → worse product), tree regrouping (identical netlist — equivalent shapes canonicalize).

6. **`target_delay` sweep** mapped abc's discrete band structure; the sweet spot (target 691) landed the best A×D point, and the design is robust — still ~2.97M at the default target.

# Session Summary

**Final: PASS (0/6782 errors), ADP = 2.936e6** (`design_best.sv` @ target_delay 691; area 3805, delay 772ps) vs baseline 4.624e6 — **−36.5%**.

## 1. Approaches tried / what worked best

- **`design.sv` (v1, broadcast):** input skew 7…14, activations broadcast per row, no de-skew registers, 19-bit accums. Failed from line 271 — diagnosed the **anti-diagonal stall boundary**: golden captures PE(i,j)'s product at edge `T+i+j`, so a mid-flight weight reload splits old/new weights along `i+j = const`. Broadcast splits by *row* → wrong. The canonical hop topology (skew i → hop j → de-skew 7−j) is **mandatory** and also flop-minimal.
- **`design.sv` (v2, canonical + 19-bit):** first PASS, 3.186e6.
- **`design_prw/prwm/best`:** per-row widths + explicit multiplier tree + row-0 specialization + dead column-7 hop removal → winner.
- **`design_csa.sv`:** 9-term carry-save MAC — best raw delay (726ps) but +336 area → 2.999e6, lost on A×D.

## 2. Biggest impact optimizations

1. **Explicit gated-shift partial-product tree** in `tpu_pe` (−400 area units, ~−10%): `a*w = Σ_{k<7} w[k]·a·2^k − w[7]·a·2^7` (exact mod 2¹⁶). Yosys's default signed `$mul` techmap bloats the cone.
2. **Exact accumulator widths:** a k-product sum fits `pw(k) = clog2(16384k+1)+1` bits → 16,17,17,18,18,18,18,19 (not 32), with a flat exact-width mesh vector (`COLW`/`rowoff` functions).
3. **Dead logic:** row-0 PE skips its adder (constant-0 input), last column has no `a_east` register.
4. **target_delay tuning:** abc maps in discrete bands; 691 was the sweet spot (default 500 also good at 2.971e6).

## 3. What didn't work / regressions

- **Prefix/Sklansky adder** (`design_pre.sv`): +70 area, +20 delay — abc re-canonicalizes equivalent functions.
- **Unsigned mul + sign corrections** (`design_umul.sv`): worse on both (4092/860).
- **Tree regrouping** (`design_alt.sv`): bit-identical netlist — equivalent shapes canonicalize; only *structurally distinct* formulations change the mapping.
- **Per-row widths alone at target 500** (`design_prw`): area *rose* (+330) — abc shifted to a faster/bigger Pareto point; needed target re-tuning to pay off.
- **Verilog gotcha (CSA bug):** `cond ? {Q{1'b0}} : $signed(x)` zero-extends the signed branch (mixed-signedness ternary) → errors of exactly +2^PNW per negative partial sum. Fixed with explicit `{{(Q-PNW){sign}}, x}`.

## 4. Lessons

- **Derive the exact cycle schedule first** — the equivalence constraint (product capture edges `T+i+j`) was forced by stall semantics; discovering it late cost ~6 steps. I should have hand-traced a mid-flight reload against the vectors before building architecture v1.
- **RTL structure only matters when it changes what abc receives** (junk size / shallowness), not when it's a rearrangement of the same function — abc canonicalizes the rest.
- **Area and delay often trade through abc's mapping**, not through RTL width reductions alone; A×D needs target sweeps *per structure* since bands are narrow and non-monotonic (691–695 good, 693/696/697 bad).
- Keep a stable "best PASS" file (`design_best.sv`) and evaluate every variant in isolation so attribution stays clean.
